===== Latency decomposition: backup service time vs. client retry wait =====
wall_ms is what the previous harness reported as 'latency'. Any gap between
wall and service is the client's own backoff sleep, not backup delay.

===== Run-level summary: service latency =====
One observation per (method, context_seed, trial). Latency statistics are
computed within each run, then averaged across runs; CIs are cluster
bootstraps over runs. Failed calls are censored (counted in failure rate,
excluded from latency), not imputed as a sentinel value.

===== Service latency vs. backup load (one observation per run x backup) =====
Does sharing a backup among more primaries raise latency, independent of
which method produced the sharing? Aggregated per (run, backup) so that
correlated calls against one loaded server count once, not thousands of times.
